# Test backlog: what we do not know yet

*Generated from every `*/subfields/*/UNKNOWNS.md`. These are candidates for `../single_agent/SCHEMA.md` that are not established. Nothing here enters the schema until its test passes on our bench at equal tokens. The full test design and the "add if" rule for each row are in the linked file. Some rows overlap across subfields (for example "nothing follows" and counterexample search); run overlapping ones as one test.*

3270 open items: 1114 High, 1368 Med, 788 Low.
